## Survey measurement increment (10 October 2026, Asia/Karachi)

Migration 052 adds internal project-scoped measurement records with explicit title, location, signed decimal value, unit and measurement date; method, evidence reference and notes begin blank. Values retain decimal text/precision without conversion, default zero or inferred business standards. Storage accepts at most six decimal places and an absolute value up to 1,000,000,000,000; these are technical bounds, not acceptance thresholds. Missing units, malformed/nonfinite numbers and future dates (Karachi business date) are denied. Method/evidence are bounded plain text; file uploads, instruments/calibration, structured survey batches and template/BOQ calculation remain separate gaps.

Assigned authorized managers create/revise Draft measurements. Administrators review with explicit method/evidence, revise or withdraw review, and archive an existing Reviewed entry. Archived measurements are immutable. Fixed project/version, exact creation retries, immutable actor/time/reason/before-after history, RLS and denied direct mutation preserve evidence; clients, unassigned/revoked/disabled managers and anonymous callers cannot use the internal register. Configurator controls retain failed forms, keep values/units/dates blank initially, gate review/archive by role and expose scoped CSV/history. Survey review does not approve contractual scope, budgets, standards or project closure.

Migration 059 adds an append-only administrator-recorded whole-request fulfilment assessment against a current Approved request decision: explicit Not fulfilled / Partially fulfilled / Fulfilled, scope coverage against the approved request, remaining work/limitations, evidence reference and assessment/correction reason. Blank or inferred business values are not accepted. Server-attributed administrator/time, sequential assessment revision, previous-assessment linkage, frozen approved-decision details and same-project/current-decision linked task history with current plan versions at assessment are retained. Corrections append new records; frozen requests and previous assessments stay unchanged. Current-prior checks serialize conflicting assessments under the project lock. Exact retries, including delayed retries after a correction, return the original record without duplicates; changed payloads cannot reuse its ID. Active-admin checks precede retry/read/write; client, worker, unassigned/revoked manager, disabled and anonymous access boundaries retain existing scope. Assigned managers can read history but cannot record assessments. Direct authenticated insert/update/delete is denied.

The change register shows the latest recorded assessment and immutable history with frozen planning evidence. The editor begins blank, retains failed inputs and uses stable exact retry IDs. Scoped history CSV includes evidence, coverage, limitations, actor/time, decision and planning snapshots; the existing application-summary CSV reports the explicit recorded assessment, or Not assessed when none exists. This is an administrator's recorded judgment, not automatic request completion, proof of executed work, commercial BOQ/budget/contract approval or client sign-off. Later planning changes do not rewrite historical snapshots. Evidence references are recorded text, not new file uploads. No reviewer, actual assignment, salary, policy, standard or target is invented; no external email/SMS/WhatsApp delivery occurs.

## DEV-021 locked approval PDF increment (18 October 2026)

Prepared from main 3c4ccf26d72be56beecb5bc4c24c7de51c437d5e. CLI scaffold 20261017232001_charter_approval_pdf.sql; migration 101 applied once. Approval endpoint automatically generates PDF from the immutable approved public snapshot, actual full name/account/server timestamp/IP/consent/note. Server-only rendering uses embedded licensed DejaVu and Noto Naskh Arabic fonts, right-to-left shaping/layout, bounded pagination, approval stamp and page numbering. No internal task/cost/margin/source-review data is included. Static PDF has no form fields; standard-reader permissions allow viewing/printing/copying and restrict modification/annotations. Lock means the retained original is immutable with hash verification and reader editing restrictions. Downloaded bytes remain verifiable against the saved SHA-256. Original immutable project evidence is the authoritative copy.

One PDF archive per actual approval, complete PDF bytea, SHA-256, exact immutable receipt hash, renderer version and actual archive actor/server time retained in the project. Existing 100 narrow production-only gateway/client JWT protects source/archive/download; no new service/admin key or secret. Source RPC checks current active own-client/company access or project-manager access; browser cannot supply final PDF bytes. Invalid hash/format/bounds rejected. ## Training attendance decisions confirmed by user (18 October 2026, 16:46 PKT)

The user has answered the three attendance questions. These decisions supersede earlier entries treating attendance authority/evidence/participant scope as unanswered; do not repeat those questions or interpret them as requiring invented business defaults. There are two types: Client training, linked to a project and usually at handover; and Internal training, for team skills and learning-hours KPI. The facilitator/trainer marks attendance. Client training is then approved by the assigned PM; Internal training is approved by the team lead/HR. Admin may view and correct all, with actual corrections retained in immutable history. No actual facilitator, PM, lead/HR reviewer or participant is assigned by this requirements update.

Required evidence: check-in and check-out time per attendee, entered by the facilitator, plus an uploaded photo or signed attendance sheet. Client training additionally requires portal confirmation by the client's authorized signatory using agreement tick, actual typed name and recorded time, following the charter-style confirmation method. Do not equate the existing text evidence reference, Draft roster membership or general daily staff attendance with these session-specific proofs. No drawn signature or paid e-signature service is requested. Do not silently add a training-PDF generation requirement from the charter feature; the user specified the confirmation method and uploaded evidence here.

Both internal and client/external participants are allowed. Select internal staff from actual user accounts; client/external attendees need only name, phone and email, with no account required. Only internal staff may receive learning-hours/KPI credit; external attendees receive none. Internal training contributes to learning-hours KPI. Retain the existing approved Training activity learning-hours source until an explicit integrated approval/credit path is implemented; prevent duplicate credits and do not award hours from a Draft roster, unapproved attendance or external attendee record. Do not infer breaks, rounding, thresholds, automatic eligibility, extra financial effects or credit for other training types without supported requirements. No external email/SMS/WhatsApp delivery is authorized.

## DEV-025 Client attendance PM review implementation checkpoint (18 October 2026)

Administrators can inspect, return or withdraw current review; approval still requires PM permission plus project assignment. No new self-approval prohibition or business threshold is inferred.

Exact same-actor retries acknowledge existing receipts; changed payload, stale previous receipt, duplicate/invalid transitions, missing/changed proof or revoked authority are guarded. Return/withdrawal permits a new reasoned submission of that receipt; new attendance or source versions make old approvals historical and require a fresh proof/submission/review. Submitted proof is validated against actual private stored objects at submission and approval. Client users gain no visibility/signing rights; no hours, activity/cost, payroll or KPI credit is created. Existing 053 documentation review remains distinct.

### DEV-027 certificate issuance and expiry increment — 19 October 2026

Continued from main 1a116c65e232b0e1ba630a649bfa211133d9c3ea / PR #186. New migration 125_competence_certificates.sql adds immutable actual issued certificates and reasoned revocations, scoped employee-own/HR/Admin SELECT RLS, no anonymous read/execute/direct authenticated writes, private eligibility/read/status/immutable helpers and four guarded register/context/issue/revoke RPCs. No actual criteria, accounts, reviewer/issuer assignments, certificates or production fixtures are seeded.

New Overtime & performance → Competence certificates explicitly loads employee-own records or current HR/Admin oversight. HR/Admin manually selects an actual assessment from current independently Approved Pass candidates, inspects exact receipt/review/version/criteria and enters a real issuance reason. Source criteria must be exact latest Active, actual employee and designated assessor current/active, actual independent assigned reviewer eligible and approval unwithdrawn, assessment uncancelled. Draft/unrecorded/unreviewed/Returned/Fail/withdrawn/stale/forged/cancelled/revoked/disabled source authority cannot issue. Assessor, reviewer and ordinary employee gain no proxy issuer power; actual current HR/Admin performs issuance. No global issuer/person is selected automatically.

Issued evidence freezes actual employee/assessor assignment and criteria/checklist, exact recorded Pass receipt and independent review, actual issuer identity/role reference/reason/server time, administrator validity/default 12 months and explicitly configured warning days. Expiry is calculated in UTC calendar months from server issuance time; warning date uses only the explicitly configured lead time, otherwise remains null. The client cannot override dates, settings or outcome. One certificate per actual assessment and exact request/account retries prevent duplicate issuance. Later source/settings/role changes do not rewrite or fabricate revocation of already-issued frozen evidence. HR/Admin can explicitly revoke with actual reason, original certificate snapshot, actor and server time; no delete, expiry rewrite, in-place replacement, automatic restoration or copied renewal is introduced.

Current/Renewal due/Expired/Revoked status is server-evaluated on refresh, with exact configured warning/expiry boundaries and revocation precedence. Scope does not expose certificates or HR candidates to unrelated staff/reviewers, clients or disabled accounts. Private certificate CSV retains full issued and reasoned revocation evidence. UI blank selections, actual reason, current scope/generation/account-away/back/unmount guards, failed-view/export clearing and immutable uncertain-request reconciliation remain. Targeted PGlite/JSDOM tests cover issuance blockers, exact retries, account/role revocation, immutable evidence, configured/default UTC dates and boundary status plus private UI/CSV cleanup. Full final-source validation, new one-time migration and exact PR/production evidence follow in the release checkpoint.

Precise next DEV-027: explicit renewal linkage/history using a separate actual current independently Approved Pass assessment and HR/Admin issuance; then issued employee skill profiles used by Configurator suggestions, with actual resource selection/assignment manual. This bounded issuance release does not yet add certificate-backed skill/profile/suggestion effects. No accreditation wording, public verification/QR service, drawn signature, certificate PDF requirement, warning threshold, automatic renewal, salary/working-hour/rounding or KPI policy is inferred. No training credit/activity/labour/cash effect. See docs/competence-certificates-guide.md.

Continued from main 96edba92cc9ebbef5bba1225468d1d1f51e6ef0d / PR #188. New migration 126_competence_certificate_renewals.sql adds append-only Linked/Withdrawn events between an actual previous issued certificate and a separate new issued certificate. New Overtime & performance → Certificate renewals explicitly loads current own/HR certificate scope, starts certificate/predecessor selections blank, and exposes own read-only linkage history or HR/Admin link/withdraw controls. No previous certificate, reviewer, issuer, employee, competency or outcome is chosen automatically.

A fresh link requires distinct certificates/assessments for the same actual employee and competency definition, previous issuance no later than new issuance, a valid unrevoked/unexpired new certificate, current exact independently Approved Pass source, active internal employee and current designated assessor/reviewer/current Active criteria. Earlier revoked/expired predecessor evidence can be retained as history without restoring or extending it. Server serialization, exact sequence/request/account checks, one current predecessor/successor and cycle rejection prevent duplicate, stale, forked or circular history. Current HR/Admin may reasonedly withdraw a mistaken link before explicitly correcting it; old link retries only acknowledge the original event and never recreate withdrawn state. Historical cleanup remains possible when the employee/source is no longer eligible.

Both full issued certificate snapshots retain actual assessment/receipt/review/employee/issuer/validity/time/reason evidence. Link-time predecessor/new status, actual linking/withdrawing account, server time, reason and canonical request remain immutable. Neither original issued record, expiry/warning date, revocation nor assessment outcome is rewritten. Linkage does not issue, automatically renew, copy results, extend validity, revoke/restore certificates or award skills/learning/pay/cash. A fresh separately completed assessment, actual independent review and HR/Admin issuance still precede linking. No renewal timing window, grace period or automatic business policy is invented.

Employee-own/HR/Admin RLS, no anonymous read/execute/direct authenticated writes and an immutable history trigger protect this scope. Two guarded RPCs provide exact scoped context/necessary matching predecessor options and reasoned link/withdraw actions. Ordinary assessors/reviewers gain no unrelated certificate access or coordinator directory. Private CSV preserves both snapshots and immutable link/correction evidence. Account/permission/generation/unmount/late-response guards clear failed private data/exports and reconcile uncertain action IDs while preserving unsaved inputs. Full validation, new one-time migration and exact PR/production evidence follow in the release checkpoint. See docs/competence-renewals-guide.md.

Renewal linkage is evidence, not a second skill or learning credit. No production business fixtures or external delivery.

## DEV-028 frozen client BOQ line revision offers — 20 October 2026

Bounded implementation: from an exact immutable Reviewed original-line proposal, Admin explicitly checks publication history and enters actual client title, terms, validity date and internal reason. Publication freezes the original accepted quotation identity/revision/version/line, original scope, reviewed Replace/Remove action and exact replacement quantity/unit price, project/company/currency and client terms. Private internal proposal/review snapshots and publication reason are retained separately from the explicit client-safe projection. Original accepted quotations, sale/budget, invoices, payments, workstreams, tasks and assignments remain unchanged.

An active own-company client with current project access chooses accept/decline; acceptance requires explicit agreement to this exact frozen revision, with actual account, server time, note and immutable published-content receipt. This follows existing BOQ offer authorization/consent, not the separate training-signatory rules. Admin cannot impersonate client acceptance. Decline requires a reason. Admin can withdraw an open offer with a client-visible reason, including obsolete scope. Accepted decisions cannot be withdrawn or overwritten as open offers. Exact same-account publication/decision retries do not duplicate evidence. Publishing or deciding rejects stale approved changes, changed baseline version/content, missing/Returned internal review, inactive access, expired validity, closed/released-handover projects and wrong company/project; withdrawal can retire obsolete published offers without approval.

One pending or Accepted publication blocks another for that proposal. After Declined/Withdrawn, Admin may explicitly publish corrected title/terms/validity against the exact latest prior publication; frozen scope cannot be edited. Changing the underlying Reviewed scope needs a separate controlled proposal-retirement/supersession workflow, not this terms-republication action. Direct writes and privileged ordinary updates/deletes cannot rewrite publication/decision evidence. The client register and CSV contain only necessary frozen client scope/terms and decision provenance; internal reasons, change evidence and reviewer snapshots are not exposed. Failed or obsolete account/company responses clear register/export access. No external delivery is sent.

Released UI after verification: Project BOQ change offers → Original BOQ line revision proposals → load actual project → Reviewed proposal → Check publication history for reviewed proposal → enter actual client fields and publish. Admin/PM/client separately use Frozen BOQ line revision offers → Load frozen BOQ line revisions; only the actual client receives approval/decline controls, Admin receives withdrawal controls, and managers receive read-only published evidence.

Approval is consent to the proposed line revision and its actual replacement quantity/unit price/terms. It does not compute project total, net credit/refund, tax/WHT settlement, rebilling or executed-work disposition, and does not apply the change to accepted scope, finance, budget or execution. No removal is represented by an invented zero-price replacement. Accepted additional-offer line revisions and dropped-product adoption remain further DEV-028 work. DEV-029 budget and DEV-030 contract integration stay separate.

Validation/release evidence follows after full regression, exact preview, one-time migration 129_boq_client_revisions.sql, PR merge and production verification.
